The **ollama divergence is the discriminating witness** throughout (its provider *key* is not value-equal to its `ProviderFamily`, so a test that passes under `anthropic` or `openai` proves nothing about canonicalization), and **round-trip retrievability is witnessed directly** — `test_captured_scope_round_trips_where_the_pre_repair_scope_does_not` in `harness-runtime/tests/test_u_mem_26_write_boundary.py`, carrying an explicit precondition anchor because *both sides of its comparison are derived from the writer* and it would otherwise stay green against a writer that repaired nothing (caught by the mutation probe, not by review). Mutation-probed: ignoring the composed `record_scope` reddens all four capture witnesses. The forward-only residual stands as **stated** at C-MEM-03 — pre-repair raw-key records are not rewritten and stay unreachable under family-scoped requests. Close-out steps below are history (pre-ratification).
- **Close-out steps.** **(1)** Do **not** land ahead of the B-86 spec-leg; the direction is the B-86 council record's, not this row's. **(2)** Once ratified, the repair is mechanical at `memory_capture.py:585-591` and its callers, witnessed by asserting that an `ollama`-written note **is** retrievable under a `local_open_weight`-scoped request of its own family (the currently-failing case), and mutation-probed by restoring the raw-key construction. **(3)** State the forward-only migration residual in the spec-leg.
- **Council: no.** The keying decision is the B-86 council record's; once the spec-leg ratifies, the repair is mechanical (the writer consumes the composed `record_scope`).
- **Cross-ref.** Surfaced by the B-86 grounding pass; direction fixed by the B-86 council record; **did** incidentally close **B-90** when the repair landed (both rows closed 2026-07-28). Arc record at `.harness/u-mem-26-impl-leg-close-2026-07-28.md`.


### B-90 · Capture-path `MemoryScope` omits `tenant` and `workload_class` — tool-captured records are tenant-unpartitioned *(surfaced at the B-86 council convening, 2026-07-28; CLOSED 2026-07-28 — U-MEM-26 impl leg)*

- **What it is.** `memory_capture.py:585-591` constructs its `MemoryScope` with only `project` / `workflow` / `provider_family` / `cli_profile` / `visibility`. `tenant` and `workload_class` are **omitted entirely**, while the composed **request** scope populates them (`harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/automatic_memory.py:194-199`).
- **Why the omission is not benign — wildcard-on-`None`.** A `None` **record** value passes all three enforcement sites: `MemoryRetriever._scope_mismatch` skips a `None` on either side (`harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_retrieval.py:377-383`); the index `_scope_matches` carries the same field list (`memory_retrieval_index.py:379-396`); and `memory_policy._scope_not_broader` guards only **non-`None`** record values (`if record_value is not None and requested_value != record_value`, `memory_policy.py:336-359`), so a `None` record tenant passes there too. **Net:** every tool-captured / episodic record is **tenant-unpartitioned** and reachable by **any** tenant's family-matching request — against the cleared threat-model mandate at `design-substrate/Spec_Memory_Substrate_v1.md:481`: *"Retrieval and injection enforce project, workflow, tenant, provider-family, CLI-profile, and visibility scope before ranking."*
- **Honest caveats.** Single-tenant local deployment is the current default posture, so **live exposure is bounded today**. And the wildcard-on-`None` semantics were, **at registration time**, load-bearing but undocumented — they are **documented as of spec v1.1** (C-MEM-03) — the B-86 spec-leg's C-MEM-03 null-semantics sentence is what documents them.
- **Current state.** `closed` 2026-07-28 at the U-MEM-26 impl leg (`registered_finding` → `open` at the B-86 spec leg → `closed` here), **incidentally by the B-89 writer repair exactly as route (1) predicted**: the run's composed `record_scope` carries `tenant` and `workload_class`, so tool-captured records are no longer tenant-unpartitioned. Witnessed per step (3) below by **three separate per-predicate assertions** in `harness-is/tests/test_memory_scope_value_domain.py` — `test_b90_tenant_denial_at_the_retriever_predicate` (`_scope_mismatch`), `test_b90_tenant_denial_at_the_index_predicate` (`_scope_matches`), and `test_b90_tenant_denial_at_the_policy_predicate` (`_scope_not_broader`) — **never one composite retrieval test**, which is precisely how the omission survived; a fourth witness, `test_b90_null_tenant_request_is_denied_at_every_predicate`, pins the wildcard-on-`None` arm. Each is mutation-probed. The cleared threat-model mandate at `design-substrate/Spec_Memory_Substrate_v1.md:481` is now conformant for the capture path. Close-out steps below are history (pre-ratification).
- **Close-out steps.** **(1) Preferred — fold into the B-89 writer-side repair:** consuming the run's composed `record_scope` brings `tenant` + `workload_class` along, so the B-89 repair will incidentally close this row when it lands. **(2)** An **independent narrow fix** (stamp the two fields into the constructed scope at `memory_capture.py:585-591`) is landable **without** the spec-leg, since it needs no contract decision — but it would be superseded by (1). Prefer (1) unless a multi-tenant deployment lands first, in which case take (2) immediately and let B-89 subsume it. **(3)** Witness either shape by asserting a tenant-A request does **not** retrieve a tenant-B tool-captured record at **all three** enforcement sites; a single-site witness passes over the other two, which is how this survived.
- **Council: no.** Conformance repair to a cleared threat-model invariant (`:481`); no cross-domain tension. Fold into the B-89 writer repair at the B-86 spec-leg's impl leg.
- **Cross-ref.** Surfaced at the B-86 council convening (Probe C's adjacent half); **incidentally closed by B-89's repair**, which landed at the U-MEM-26 impl leg 2026-07-28. Arc record at `.harness/u-mem-26-impl-leg-close-2026-07-28.md`.


### B-91 · Report-only degraded-serve `failure_class` / `policy.decision` semantics for the scope-mismatch and `not_derived` reason families *(surfaced by the merge-gate spec-conformance lens on the C-MEM-19 emitter-attrs arc, 2026-07-29; CLOSED 2026-07-29 — Q1 policy_denial applied (R2-converged: class follows the family relation, not the conjunct), Q2 keep-as-is)*

- **What it is.** The B-83 degraded-serve emitter's **report-only** branch (`degraded.rendered is None`, `harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/lifecycle/llm_dispatch.py`) fires on **three** reason families, not one, and they do not share a telemetry class: **(1)** the scope-mismatch conjuncts (`provider_scope_mismatch` / `provider_family_scope_mismatch`); **(2)** genuine `MemoryAccessModeDenialReason` values carried verbatim from `prompt_packet_fallback_denial` — `no_supported_mode` / `token_budget_empty` (`harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/memory_context.py:134-138`); **(3)** the `not_derived` / `prompt_packet_denial_undetermined` sentinels (`memory_context.py:48-52`). A single rationale covering all three was written into the emitter docstring at PR #1151 and was **false for family (2)**, which is what the merge-gate spec-conformance lens caught.
- **Family (2) is CLOSED at PR #1151.** It now carries `failure_class=POLICY_DENIAL`, mirroring the **primary** DENIAL span, which sets exactly that class for exactly those values (`memory_context.py:210-216`) off a real `select_memory_access_mode` run (`memory_context.py:545-549`). One decision reported by two emitters must not carry two classes. The discrimination is carried as **data** on the `_DegradedMemoryServe` carrier (`policy_denial`, set from membership in the closed `MemoryAccessModeDenialReason` value domain at the site that knows the reason's provenance), never re-derived by string-matching in the emitter.
- **Q1 — should family (1) carry `POLICY_DENIAL` too? DECIDED 2026-07-29: YES.** The deciding text is the C-MEM-13 **recording-surface** paragraph at `design-substrate/Spec_Memory_Substrate_v1.md:511` — the withholding is recorded on the C-MEM-19 surface *"whose declared coverage already includes standard memory tool calls and policy denial"*. That sentence earns its "no new coverage item needed" by pointing at the **existing** list (`:674-683`), and of its two candidate items a withheld exposure is definitionally **not a tool CALL** — the schemas never reach the model — so *"Policy denial"* (`:683`) is the item being invoked. `memory.failure_class` is the only required attribute (`:687-698`) whose value domain carries that classification, and the failure-class invariant at `:703` uses the **identical phrase**; reading the coverage item and the vocabulary as unrelated would leave the coverage item with no carrier at all. That vocabulary is scoped by **outcome kind** (policy denial / path violation / IO failure / serialization failure / provider adapter failure / retrieval empty-result), never by *which component decided* — which settles the genuinely-open part: a **contract-mandated** withhold and a **resolver's** denial are one telemetry class. Corroborating: the withhold is a `MUST NOT` at `:509`, an invariant at `:523`, a C-MEM-14 exposure qualification at `:559`, and `no_memory_access` — what both branches report — is itself *"a valid policy outcome"* at `:522`.
- **Q1 SCOPE REFINED 2026-07-29 by out-of-family Codex R1 (two [P2]s, both upheld).** The first application set the class on **both** scope conjuncts unconditionally, which over-claimed on two counts — and the arc's own truthfulness principle (never report a determination that was not made) is what condemns it. **[P2-1] the IDENTITY conjunct carries no class at all *(SUPERSEDED by the R2 convergence below — at HEAD the relation IS resolved before the identity return and a confirmed cross-family rebind DOES carry the class)*.** It fires whenever the provider **key** moved, including an `openai`-composed context rebound to `codex` — one family, two keys — where C-MEM-13, which withholds only across **families**, mandates nothing whatsoever; the branch never resolves a family, so it cannot have confirmed the condition the `POLICY_DENIAL` claim rests on. It is a **stale-context safety guard**, and `memory.policy.decision` still names it verbatim. **[P2-2] the FAMILY conjunct carries the class only on a CONFIRMED mismatch.** `_packet_scope_matches_dispatch_family` returned `False` for an absent `record_scope`, an out-of-domain scope family, or an unregistered provider key — states where the relationship is **UNKNOWN**, not known-unequal — so the unconditional flag reported **fail-closed validation** as an operator policy decision. The Q1 reading above is unchanged and still governs the case it actually addressed: a **confirmed** cross-family withhold, both families resolved and unequal. **Discrimination mechanism:** `_packet_scope_family_relation` (new) returns a tri-state `_ScopeFamilyRelation` — `MATCH` / `CONFIRMED_MISMATCH` / `UNVERIFIABLE` — over the **same** existing `provider_family_for_scope_check` + `canonical_scope_family` authorities, no second policy path; `_packet_scope_matches_dispatch_family` is now a pure projection of it (`is MATCH`), so both withholding callers behave **identically** to before and only the telemetry class changed.
- **Q2 — does `not_derived` belong on `memory.policy.decision` at all? DECIDED 2026-07-29: KEEP AS IS** (verbatim; **no code change**). `memory.policy.decision` is a **required** attribute on every memory span (`:694`), so *omitting* it on the one branch where no gate closed would drop a required attribute exactly where a reader most needs to know the decision axis was empty. Mapping to the primary DENIAL span's generic `denied` fallback is the worse option: it asserts a denial **nobody issued** — the same false accusation the per-reason [P2-e] split exists to prevent. Verbatim is truthful on **both** halves of the B-87/B-91 principle: the sentinel names **no gate** (defined distinct from every `MemoryAccessModeDenialReason`, `memory_context.py:48-52`, so it can never be misread as one), while the span still reports `denial` / `no_memory_access` / `record_count=0`, so the degradation is never hidden.
- **No contract defect either way.** C-MEM-19 requires `memory.policy.decision` on every memory span and does **not** enumerate which spans owe a `failure_class`; every answer on the table was conformant, before and after the flip. This row was about telemetry **truthfulness**, not conformance — which is why it registered rather than halted, and why closing it needed a spec **reading** rather than a spec **amendment**.
- **Applied 2026-07-29 (close), as refined by Codex R1 and converged by Codex R2 the same day.** `_degraded_serve_disposition` sets `_DegradedMemoryServe.policy_denial` on exactly the report-only outcomes where a policy determination was **actually reached**: a selector-returned `MemoryAccessModeDenialReason`, and a **confirmed** cross-family withhold (`_ScopeFamilyRelation.CONFIRMED_MISMATCH`) — under **either** scope conjunct. R1 had corrected the first application's unconditional flag to an unconditional `False` on the **identity** branch; R2 found that under-reports by the mirror amount, since an `anthropic`-composed context rebound to `openai` resolves both families, finds them **unequal**, and is a confirmed C-MEM-13 withhold that the identity return exited before ever evaluating — unclassified purely by **test order**. **Converged rule:** `policy_denial` ⟺ `CONFIRMED_MISMATCH`, a function of the family **relation** alone, never of which conjunct fired; the relation is now resolved **once, before** the identity test (it reads only `record_scope` + `provider_name`, neither touched by that test, so no ordering hazard — two dict lookups on an already-degraded path). It stays **unset** on a **same-family** identity rebind, on every `UNVERIFIABLE` family state under either conjunct, and on the sentinels — three shapes of *nobody determined this*. The emitter itself is unchanged, since it already reads the carrier flag. Q2 changed no code. The `#1151`-era emitter docstring, which held the question open, now cites the decisions and the R1 refinement. **Witnesses stay discriminating** per close-out step (3), all four cells pinned: `test_u_mem_26_cross_family_ollama_dispatch_withholds_tools_and_packet` (the confirmed case — scope family `openai`, dispatch `ollama` → `local_open_weight`, **both resolve** and are unequal) asserts `failure_class` **present**; `test_b83_provider_rebound_context_is_reported_but_never_served` (`anthropic`-composed → `openai` — **cross**-family) asserts it **present** on the **identity** guard, while `test_b91_same_family_provider_rebind_is_not_a_policy_denial` (`codex`-composed → `openai`, **one** family) asserts it **absent** on that same guard — the R2 pair pinning the class to the relation rather than to the branch; `test_b83_absent_record_scope_fails_closed_to_report_only` and `test_b86_unregistered_provider_key_fails_closed_against_local_scope` assert it **absent** on the two `UNVERIFIABLE` shapes; `test_b83_undervied_eligibility_reports_not_derived_not_a_gate_denial` asserts it **absent** on the sentinel and additionally pins the Q2 verbatim value. **Mutation probes, all killing** (two re-run at the R2 convergence, on top of three at the R1 refinement and four at the first application): reverting the family construction to an unconditional `True` reddens **both** `UNVERIFIABLE` witnesses; dropping the `CONFIRMED_MISMATCH` `True` reddens the U-MEM-26 cross-family witness; R2 (a) forcing the identity construction back to a relation-blind `False` reddens the **cross-family** rebound witness (`KeyError: 'memory.failure_class'`); R2 (b) forcing it unconditionally `True` reddens the **same-family** rebind witness. **No spec amendment**: a telemetry-truthfulness reading of already-cleared text, decided inside the C-MEM-19 closed enum (attribute **values** only, no new members).
- **Council: no.** A bounded telemetry-semantics decision inside an already-cleared contract, with no cross-domain tension to surface. It does require a careful spec **reading** (C-MEM-13 `:511` / `:523` + C-MEM-14 `:559` against C-MEM-19), so it is not mechanical — but one grounded pass settles both questions.
- **Cross-ref.** Surfaced by the merge-gate spec-conformance lens on PR #1151 (the C-MEM-19 `cli_profile` + `policy.decision` emitter-attrs arc); family (2) closed in that same PR. Adjacent registered telemetry-vocabulary row: **B-88** (the closed C-MEM-19 `MemoryTelemetryFailureClass` enum has no input-validation member).
### B-92 · C-MEM-10 promotion eligibility of records captured during a cross-family fallback leg *(named open question carried forward at Spec_Memory_Substrate_v1.md v1.1 change-note "Named open question carried forward, not discharged." and U-MEM-26's "Out of scope for this unit:" list; Class 1 fork FILED 2026-07-29; CLOSED 2026-07-29 — U-MEM-27 impl leg)*

- **What it is.** The memory spec's **own** named-but-undischarged open question, now filed as a Class 1 fork at `.harness/class_1_fork_c_mem_10_cross_family_promotion_eligibility.md`. Since U-MEM-26 (PR #1148) capture writes under the run's **composed** scope, so a cross-family fallback leg lands an `anthropic`-family record while the dispatch that produced its content ran on `openai`. Whether such a record may be **promoted** — and thereafter injected back into future runs under the primary's family — is the C-MEM-10 question the change-note paragraph **"Named open question carried forward, not discharged."** of `design-substrate/Spec_Memory_Substrate_v1.md` explicitly carries forward: *"whether records captured during a cross-family fallback leg are promotion-eligible under C-MEM-10 … v1.1 neither resolves nor forecloses it"*. `design-substrate/Implementation_Plan_Memory_Substrate_v1.md` lists it out-of-scope for U-MEM-26 with the same carry, at that unit's **"Out of scope for this unit:"** subsection. *(Cite convention, Codex R3 [P2-2]: this row cites the memory spec by contract ID + section name + byte-verified quoted fragment, never by line, per that spec's own v1.1 instruction — "Cite this spec by contract ID and section name, not by line, in any text authored after v1.1." Code cites keep `file:line` and were verified at HEAD `6ab41d7f`, against which no code file differs from the `dd2a8c1a` grounding HEAD.)*
- **Witnessed, not inferred.** A shipped test asserts the behaviour: `test_u_mem_26_capture_is_unaffected_by_the_cross_family_withholding` (`harness-runtime/tests/test_automatic_memory_runtime.py:515`) drives an `anthropic`-primary / `openai`-candidate dispatch and asserts at `:579` that `captured.envelope.scope.provider_family == ProviderFamily.ANTHROPIC.value`.
- **Current behaviour: silent at both entry points — but ZERO live auto-promotion exposure at HEAD.** *(The first draft's exposure claims were corrected by an out-of-family Codex R1 pass, 2026-07-29 — one [P1] and one [P2], both upheld on direct re-read.)* **(1) The hint path — the candidate-side family is producer-defined, hence undefined today.** `_candidate_from_hint` (`harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/memory_promotion.py:296`) builds risk flags from the *hint* with `source_scope=record.envelope.scope` (`:626-629`), but the **candidate** side that `_family_escapes_source` (`:715-743`) compares is `hint.suggested_scope` (`:682`) — a **required, caller-supplied** field (`:191`) with no default and no derivation from the record. A producer mirroring the composed run scope supplies `anthropic` (no flag); a producer supplying the **dispatched** family already trips `CROSS_SCOPE` (`:683`). *Which* convention a producer must supply is itself part of what C-MEM-10 owes, and cannot be read off code that has no producer. `_auto_promote_allowed` (`:776-790`) carries no family term and no risk-flag term. **(2) The model-facing tool path — not a live auto-promotion exposure, for two independent reasons.** `MemoryToolExecutor._propose_promotion` (`memory_tool_executor.py:399-435`) serves the C-MEM-14 `memory.propose_promotion` tool (C-MEM-14 Contract, tool table), hardcodes `confidence=HIGH` (`:423`), and computes `risk_flags=_promotion_risk_flags(source)` (`:425`), which inspects **only** `redaction_state` (`:882-885`) — that no-scope-comparison point stands. What does **not** stand is reachability for a *captured* record. **(a)** The record kind is denied before a candidate is constructed: `_read_retrievable_record_by_ref` (`:554-567`) routes the source through `resolve_retrieval`, and `_record_kind_allowed` (`harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_policy.py:327-333`) denies any kind outside `eligible_record_kinds`; the **sole** production policy `_policy_from_config` (`automatic_memory.py:532-557`) — the only *enabled* `MemoryPolicyDocument` built anywhere under `src/` — pins that set to `_retrievable_kinds()` (`:560-569`), the seven promoted/derived kinds, **excluding every kind capture writes** (`EPISODIC_RUN`/`EPISODIC_TURN`/`TOOL_EVENT`/`COMPACTION_EVENT`, `memory_tool_executor.py:769-775`) — covering both the witness's `EPISODIC_TURN` and `memory.write_note`'s own `TOOL_EVENT` output (`memory_capture.py:471-473`) — so the call raises `MemoryToolExecutionDeniedError` (`:565-566`) *before* candidate construction at `:417`. **(b)** An eligible record could not auto-promote either: `_policy_from_config:542` pins `promotion_decision=PROPOSE_SEMANTIC` while `_promotion_auto_allowed` (`:897-903`) returns True only for `PROMOTE_SEMANTIC`/`PROMOTE_PROCEDURAL`, so every candidate on this path takes the `propose_for_review` branch (`:456`) with `review_required=True` — **a branch that is durable but inactive, not ephemeral** *(the R2 correction of the first draft's "review, not durability")*: `propose_for_review` (`memory_promotion.py:328-352`) calls `_persist_decision` with `status=PROPOSED`, and `_persist_decision` (`:451-506`) writes **both** the semantic/procedural record (`write_record`, `:498`) **and** a durable C-MEM-08 operation entry (`append_memory_operation`, `:499-506`). What review withholds is **activation**, not persistence — a `PROPOSED` record is excluded from retrieval by construction (`_INACTIVE_STATUS_REASONS["proposed"]` → `RetrievalExclusionReason.PROPOSED`, `harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_retrieval.py:65` / `:71-77`) — and only `approve` / `edit_and_approve` (`:354-390` / `:416-449`) flip it to `ACTIVE`, neither consulting any family term.
- **What the question actually is, stated exactly.** Not "a live tool auto-promotes cross-family content today", but: **(i)** *future retrievable-kind records* — `eligible_record_kinds` is a C-MEM-09 **policy** field, and a **second-order** case needs no policy change at all, since a semantic/procedural record already promoted out of a cross-family leg is an eligible kind by construction and carries the same unrecorded provenance forward; **(ii)** *future policy configs* setting `PROMOTE_SEMANTIC`/`PROMOTE_PROCEDURAL` with `ReviewMode.AUTOMATIC`, which C-MEM-09 explicitly permits; **(iii)** the *first caller* to populate `promotion_candidates`. **None of the three is individually sufficient, and the row states the combinations rather than letting "a single policy field" imply otherwise** *(Codex R6 [P2-2], upheld by direct read at `1c99e208`; the earlier framing was true of candidate-**reachability**, not of active **promotion**)*: the gate functions are conjunctions and one conjunct is **already satisfied in production**. **(a)** admitting a capture-written kind into `eligible_record_kinds` alone buys **candidate-reachability only** — `_policy_from_config` still pins `PROPOSE_SEMANTIC` (`automatic_memory.py:542`), so `_promotion_auto_allowed` (`memory_tool_executor.py:897-903`) is False and the record lands as a durable, retrieval-excluded `PROPOSED`. **(b)** switching the decision to `PROMOTE_SEMANTIC`/`PROMOTE_PROCEDURAL` alone buys **active auto-promotion of already-eligible kinds only** — exactly the second-order case — since capture-written kinds are still denied before a candidate exists. **(c)** `ReviewMode.AUTOMATIC` buys **nothing: it is already set** (`automatic_memory.py:555`), so only the decision conjunct of `_promotion_auto_allowed` is unmet at HEAD (and the other two modes are live foreclosures a deployment could re-assert — `OPERATOR_REQUIRED` forces review at `:889-890`, `FORBIDDEN` denies the tool at `:406-407`). So on the **tool path** live auto-promotion of a *captured* record requires **(a) ∧ (b)**, both hard-coded side by side at `_policy_from_config` (`:542` and `:556`) and drawn from no `config.memory` field — today a code change, though C-MEM-09 **policy** fields by contract, which is why the gap is real regardless. On the **hint path** it requires **(iii) ∧ (b) ∧ non-`LOW` confidence** (`memory_promotion.py:786-787`; `PROMOTE_PROCEDURAL` specifically for a `PROCEDURAL_UPDATE` kind, `:788-790`) **∧ an actuator that does not exist**: the only `PromotionDecisionService.approve` call site anywhere under `src/` is the tool path's (`memory_tool_executor.py:449`), `extract_from_records` is reached solely from `CompactionSafetyHook.extract_candidates` (`memory_compaction_safety.py:153-159`) whose sibling `complete_compaction` (`:161-209`) persists **dispositions** and never promotes, and `CompactionSafetyHook` itself has no production caller at all (only its definition and its `harness_runtime/__init__.py` export). On that path `auto_promote_allowed=True` is computed, persisted, and **actuated by nothing**. The **contract** gap is unconditioned by all of it — C-MEM-10 has no provenance term at any policy setting. The §1 witness stands regardless: it pins the **capture-side** fact, which is what makes the question well-posed at all.
- **Prerequisite finding — on the composed-scope path (the U-MEM-26 production path), the pipeline cannot discriminate even if policy said to.** *(Scoped by Codex R2; the first draft asserted an exhaustive `provider` data-flow that holds only on that path.)* `EpisodicMemoryCapture` takes `record_scope: MemoryScope | None = None` (`memory_capture.py:253`) and `_scope_for_record` (`:1048-1086`) branches on it. **Composed path (scope supplied):** the envelope is the run's composed scope (`:1074`) through canonicalize-or-deny (`:1083-1086`), and the per-dispatch `provider` never reaches the envelope — this is **production**, since the automatic-memory runtime always supplies a composed scope keyed to `fallback_chain.primary.family.value` (`automatic_memory.py:211-221`, family at `:217`, threaded at `:504-512` / `:511`), and it is the path the witness exercises. **Residual path (no scope):** `_scope_for_record` builds the scope itself with `provider_family=provider` (`:1075-1082`, field at `:1079`), so such a record **does** retain its producing family and the exhaustive claim does not hold for it — but it retains that family by **partitioning the record under it**, the very per-turn re-derivation B-89 named as a defect and B-90 / U-MEM-26 repaired, so the cross-family question **does not arise there at all** (a record written under the producing family is not a foreign-family record inside a primary's partition) and there is nothing for C-MEM-10 to discriminate. **Force undiminished:** the path where the question is well-posed is exactly the path that cannot answer it, and it is the production path. On that path, `capture_turn_completion` writes `content` with **no** provider and **no** dispatched-model field (`:407-422`); `provider` / `model` reach only the **C-MEM-08 ledger row** (`:694-707`; `MemoryOperationPayload.provider` at `:1006`, `memory_refs=(memory_id,)` at `:1010`) and the **C-MEM-19 span** attributes (`:686-688`, `:714-716`) — and promotion reads neither (`_hints_from_record` reads `record.content` alone, `:545-560`). So readings B and C below are not merely unbuilt for composed-scope records (every record the production path writes), they are **unrepresentable**, and surfacing the discriminator is itself contract-altitude work, X-AL-3-blocked at Phase 7.
- **Second grounded correction — `risk_flags` gates nothing today.** A repo-wide grep finds it only at its definition, its two construction sites, and its persistence; `_review_required` (`memory_promotion.py:762-773`) never consults it and `_auto_promote_allowed` reads `review_required`, not flags. Any flag-based reading must therefore state the **gate** as well as the flag or it lands inert.
- **Net, corrected.** The permissiveness is **structural, not yet exercised**: live auto-promotion exposure at HEAD is **nil** on the composed-scope production path — the tool path is closed twice over (by record kind and by policy decision), and the hint path is **unfed** (the sole production caller passes `promotion_candidates=()`, `automatic_memory.py:273`; the extractor is otherwise reached only from compaction safety, `memory_compaction_safety.py:159`) with its candidate convention undefined. What is real *now* is the contract gap — which **no single named change converts into a live exposure** (Codex R6 [P2-2]; the combinations are **(a) ∧ (b)** on the tool path, with **(c)** `ReviewMode.AUTOMATIC` already satisfied in production, or the hint producer **∧ (b) ∧ an actuator that does not exist**). What a single change *does* buy is the **transition below it** — (a) makes captured records candidate-reachable and durably `PROPOSED`; (b) makes the second-order case actively auto-promoting — and each such transition happens **with no further review**, because no gate on any of these paths has a family term to consult. That is why this is a Class 1 **spec** question and not a Phase 7 bug, and why the **urgency** is low while the **necessity** is not: nothing is leaking today, the last step to leaking is a two-field policy change nothing forbids, and the contract has no position to fall back on when it happens.
- **Three readings (fork §4).** **(A)** promotion-eligible unchanged, status quo made **explicit** — cheapest (one C-MEM-10 paragraph, zero code, zero discriminator); grounded in C-MEM-03 §"`MemoryScope.provider_family` value domain and derivation" (the run's partition is a run-level identity the fallback chain preserves), the B-86/B-89 capture ratification (C-MEM-13 §"Cross-family withholding of standard memory tools", "a different authorship class"), and the fact that the content passed the same capture pipeline as any other record. **Codex R4 [P1] correction to that second bullet, upheld by direct read and stated rather than quietly repaired:** a first draft added *"promotion confers durability on content the harness holds, and grants the foreign candidate no new reach"*, and **both halves were wrong — promotion is a reach transition, not a durability one.** The captured record is **already durable**: `_capture` writes it to the canonical store (`self._store.write_record(record)`, `memory_capture.py:730`) *before* its C-MEM-08 row, deliberately (`:721-729`). What promotion adds is **injectability** — every capture-written kind sits outside the sole production policy's `eligible_record_kinds` (`_policy_from_config` pins it to `_retrievable_kinds()`, `automatic_memory.py:556`, defined `:560-569`), and a `PROPOSED` promotion output is retrieval-excluded by construction (`memory_retrieval.py:71-77`), so **only** an `ACTIVE` semantic/procedural record is retrievable and therefore injectable into a future run. Foreign-produced content **does** gain new reach at promotion — content that could not enter a future run's context becomes content that can, under the primary's family attribution — which is exactly the Memory threat model §Threats channel *"Cross-run prompt-injection persistence through promoted semantic/procedural memory."* and exactly the §Invariants transition *"Model-authored notes are episodic by default and cannot become injectable semantic memory without policy and evidence."* What gains nothing is the foreign **provider** (no capability, no tool, no boundary crossing it did not already have) — that, and only that, is the true residue of the C-MEM-13 carve-out. **A's honest form is therefore "the reach transition is *acceptable* because the composed scope is the run's declared identity", not "there is no reach transition"**: A loses the only rationale that claimed the question was *benign* rather than merely *acceptable*, and B's contract argument is correspondingly **strengthened**. **(B)** flag cross-family-produced candidates with a `CROSS_SCOPE`-equivalent risk flag **plus a stated gate** (review-required, never auto-promotable) — preserves eligibility, removes silent auto-promotion, requires the discriminator; grounded in the threat model's own provenance axis (its §Threats opener and its cross-run-prompt-injection-persistence bullet, and its model-authored-notes §Invariant). **(C)** refuse promotion outright — strongest isolation, still requires the discriminator, and forecloses the continuity value the B-86 council explicitly preserved.
- **Discriminator options (fork §5; needed by B and C).** **(i) A new content/envelope field at C-MEM-03 altitude** — **two variants with materially different blast radii, split at Codex R6 [P2-1] (upheld by direct read at `1c99e208`) because the R4 accounting charged the identity-movement consequence to the whole of (i) when it belongs to the content variant alone.** *Both* variants pay a closed-schema amendment (`MemoryRecordEnvelope` is `ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)`, `harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_record_envelope.py:129`; and at contract altitude either reverses v1.1's own *"zero new field"* posture, change-note **"Sections preserved verbatim at v1.1."**, one delta later), *both* are derived at **one central site** (R9 below), and *both* are **forward-only** absent a back-fill (which would be option (ii) run offline, inheriting its whole unresolvable set). **Codex R9 [P2-1] correction, upheld by direct read at `26720e8e` and stated rather than quietly repaired:** the pre-R9 text charged both variants with *"touching every capture writer"* (fork §5: *"Six `capture_*` methods plus `_capture` / `_record` thread the new value"*) — **false on two counts.** There are **seven** `capture_*` methods on `EpisodicMemoryCapture` (`memory_capture.py:294`/`:337`/`:382`/`:439`/`:487`/`:534`/`:585`; class at `:241`), not six, and **none needs to thread anything**: each already takes `provider: str | None` and passes it to `_capture` (`:321`/`:366`/`:423`/`:471`/`:518`/`:569`/`:615`), which threads it into `_record` (`:649-658`). `_record` (`:1015-1046`) already holds **both** inputs the predicate needs — the call's raw `provider` (`:1025`) and the record's **resolved** scope from `_scope_for_record` (`:1038-1042`, defined `:1048-1086`) — so the derivation is a **single hoist inside `_record`**, with zero per-method threading and zero public signature change; and an optional field defaulted to the tri-state *unknown* leaves the other three `MemoryRecordEnvelope` construction sites untouched (`memory_promotion.py:844`, `native_memory_adapter.py:463`, `memory_compaction_safety.py:327`) and lets a pre-amendment envelope still validate under `extra="forbid"`. **The leveling is unmoved** — third cost struck from (i) in three rounds (R6 identity movement, R8 the family value domain, R9 write-side reach), but on an axis (ii) had already won at **zero**, leaving untouched the three that carry (ii)'s side: existing records, aggregate-run provenance, comparison-basis flexibility. What it corrects is the cost's **kind**: (i)'s write-side price is a **contract** obligation, never a seven-signature refactor. **Codex R8 [P2-2] correction, upheld by direct read at `d9907c24` and stated rather than quietly repaired — the remaining cost, a second family-valued field beside `MemoryScope.provider_family` plus the C-MEM-03 value-domain reconciliation, was charged to all of (i) and belongs to one PAYLOAD shape only.** Option (i) varies along **two** axes: **placement** (content vs envelope, the R6 split) and **payload** (a family *value* vs the boolean `captured_cross_family` the option's own opening sentence offers and nothing then costed). **(i-envelope-bool)** stores a **tri-state** predicate (true / false / unknown, the B-91 idiom) derived at capture by canonicalizing the call's own `provider` through the *existing* house authorities (`provider_family_for_scope_check`, `harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/lifecycle/cross_family_cost_tag.py:101`; `canonical_scope_family` / `resolve_scope_family`, `harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/memory_scope_family.py:50` / `:62`) and comparing it against the record's own composed `scope.provider_family` — **no** new value domain, **no** second family-valued field, hence no one-source-of-truth hazard (a predicate *over* that field is a dependent of it, not a rival authority). It still pays the closed-schema addition, the **one** central derivation in `_record` (R9 [P2-1]), and forward-only: **not a cheaper schema change — the same schema change with a narrower contract surface behind it.** It **must** be a tri-state, not a `bool`: `provider` is `str | None` at every `capture_*` signature (`memory_capture.py:302` / `:347` / `:396`), `provider_family_for_scope_check` returns `None` for an **unregistered** key and requires scope-boundary callers to fail closed on it, and a composed scope's `provider_family` may itself be `null` (the C-MEM-03 unpartitioned wildcard), against which "cross-family" is undefined — storing `false` there would record a determination the writer never made, the permissive-by-silence defect this fork exists to close. **What it cannot answer:** *which* family produced the content (so reading B's flag semantics are bounded to present/absent, and a later family-discriminating policy needs a second amendment at the family payload's price); aggregate-run provenance (unchanged); and it bakes the comparison basis in at write time. **Net, honestly: on reading B as this filing argues it — a risk *flag* plus a gate, i.e. a present/absent signal — (i-envelope-bool) is plausibly the cheapest *adequate* discriminator**, which is R8's honest discharge rather than a restored Q2 recommendation: it changes (i)'s internal shape only, leaving untouched the three axes on which (ii) wins (already-written records, zero write-side reach, aggregate-run provenance). **(i-content)** additionally **moves identities**: `compute_memory_content_hash(content)` digests the canonicalized **content mapping alone** (`memory_record_envelope.py:207-210` over `canonicalize_memory_content`, `:189-204`) and `derive_memory_id(tier, kind, content_hash)` (`:213-222`) composes the id from it — as C-MEM-03 §Invariants states ("`content_hash` is computed over canonical serialized content excluding derived indexes.") — so every newly written record of every kind **except** `EPISODIC_RUN` (whose id is `run_id`-derived, `memory_capture.py:1100-1109`) changes identity, needing a forward-only statement. **(i-envelope)** moves **none**: `_record` (`memory_capture.py:1015-1046`) computes `content_hash` at `:1027` and `memory_id` at `:1028` **before** building the envelope around them (`:1030-1044`), and the store re-derives the same way (`expected_hash = compute_memory_content_hash(self.content)`, `harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_store.py:95`). *(This also corrects a drifted cite carried since R4, which anchored `MemoryScope`'s hash-inertness at `memory_capture.py:574-575` — a `source_ref=` / `run_id=` argument pair inside `capture_failure_observation`; the property is real, the anchors are the three named here.)* **(i-envelope) is therefore the real contender and (i-content) is strictly dominated by it. Codex R7 [P2] correction, upheld by direct read at `d8df7647` and stated rather than quietly repaired — the R3/R6 multi-writer penalty charged to (i-envelope) is FALSE.** The mechanics R6 recited are correct: `capture_run_close` (`:337-380`) does not patch a stored record but goes through `_record`, which builds a **complete, fresh** envelope including a re-computed `scope=self._scope_for_record(...)` (`:1038-1042`), and `MemoryStore.write_record` writes the **whole** canonicalized record — envelope *and* content — to the single `run.json` (`memory_store.py:178-188`; `EPISODIC_RUN` takes the `_write_file_atomically` branch at `:186` against `_run_record_path`, `:273-274` / `:290-295`). **The inference from them is wrong.** Envelope and content being written *together, from one call*, is exactly why an envelope provenance field does **not** go stale: the stored `content["event_type"]` says `run_close`, and the field would carry **that same call's** `provider` (the argument `_capture` threads into `_record` at `:649-658`), so the overwrite **preserves** provenance for the version that is stored — the identical semantics (ii-a) reaches *indirectly* by selecting the row `event_type` names. "Records nothing about which writer set it" is not a defect when the field describes the version it ships inside; the reader promotes **that** version, not the run's history. And the two diverge in exactly one place — the R7 [P1] case below — where **(i-envelope) reads the stored version's own provider and (ii-a) reads a stale one**, so on the multi-writer kind the asymmetry **inverts** in (i-envelope)'s favour. **(ii) A C-MEM-08 ledger join at promotion time** — **read-side only, no schema change**: the join key already exists — though it is the capture **`idempotency_key`**, **not** `memory_refs` and **not** the bare `action_id` (see (ii-a)); `MemoryOperationEntry` (C-MEM-08 Contract) carries `provider: string | null` and `memory_refs: list<memory_id>`, and the raw-key→family normalization reuses the existing `provider_family_for_scope_check` / `canonical_scope_family` authorities. **Codex R3 [P2-1] correction, upheld by direct read and stated rather than quietly repaired:** the first draft's premise that *"capture writes exactly one row per record"* is **false for `EPISODIC_RUN`**. `_memory_id_for` (`harness-runtime/src/harness_runtime/memory_capture.py:1100-1109`) derives an `EPISODIC_RUN` `memory_id` from the **`run_id` alone** — content-independent — so `capture_run_start` (`:294-335`) and `capture_run_close` (`:337-380`) write **two** capture rows against **one** `memory_id`, and the module says so where it exports the remedy: `capture_operation_action_id` (`:68-77`), *"run-start and run-close share a single EPISODIC_RUN `memory_id`, so `memory_refs` cannot tell them apart"* (and again at the repair-row probe, `:857-861`). Each `capture_*` method takes its **own** per-call `provider` (`:302` / `:347`; the runtime supplies the run-start selection at `automatic_memory.py:336`), so post-fallback the two rows can carry **different provider keys** and a `memory_refs`-keyed join is **not a function** — it classifies provenance by whichever row the scan selected. *Honest scoping, same footing as the rest of this row:* `capture_run_close` has **no production caller at HEAD** (sole call site `harness-runtime/tests/test_memory_capture.py:151`), so today every stored `EPISODIC_RUN` record does carry exactly one row — the ambiguity is **structural, not yet exercised**, and contract text must not rest on the accident. **Resolution, two sub-options.** **(ii-a) event-qualified join [recommended]** — key the join on the capture **`idempotency_key`**, itself composed from the capture `action_id`, which is composed from the record's *own declared writer*: `Identifier(f"idempotent:{capture_operation_action_id(stored_capture_event_type(record.content), memory_id)}")`, i.e. `idempotent:capture:{event_kind}:{memory_id}`. Both inner halves are **already exported**, and their docstrings name this exact discrimination as the reason: `stored_capture_event_type` (`memory_capture.py:1149-1163`) is *"the record's own statement of WHICH event wrote it — the discriminator a reader needs when one `memory_id` is shared by several events"*. Because the record is overwritten by its later writer, its `content["event_type"]` names the event that wrote **the version being promoted**, which is precisely the row whose `provider` is that content's provenance. **Codex R4 [P2] correction, upheld by direct read at `ca0cc5a2` and stated rather than quietly repaired:** the R3 formulation ended *"the lookup is exact-match single-row and the ledger's `action_id` idempotency makes '>1 row' unreachable rather than merely unlikely"* — and **there is no `action_id` idempotency in the ledger.** `append_memory_operation` (`harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_operation_ledger.py:522-545`) dedups by **`idempotency_key` alone** (`:532-539`) — equal key + equal 18-field equivalence payload → `IDEMPOTENT_NOOP`; equal key + divergent payload → `MemoryOperationIdempotencyConflictError` — while `action_id` is a caller-supplied field with **no** ledger-enforced uniqueness, so two entries may share one `action_id` under different idempotency keys and an `action_id`-keyed join is **not** guaranteed single-row. What makes the join well-defined is that the capture writer derives the key **deterministically** from the id (`idempotency_key=Identifier(f"idempotent:{action_id}")`, `memory_capture.py:999`, the sole construction site for every `capture_*` method), so the key **is reconstructible at promotion time from the stored record alone**, and once reconstructed the ledger guarantees **at most one** occupying entry. Two consequences the contract must carry: **(1) state the join on the key, not on the id**; and **(2) qualify on `operation_kind == CAPTURE`** — writer namespacing (`capture:...` here vs promotion's `promotion:...` at `memory_promotion.py:523-528`, which shares the *identical* `idempotent:{action_id}` spelling) is a convention across producers, not a ledger invariant, so the matched row must be **confirmed** to be a capture row, never assumed to be one. **Codex R7 [P1] correction, upheld by direct read at `d8df7647` and stated rather than quietly repaired — a matched row is not, by itself, a row about *this version of* the record, so (ii-a) owes a third consequence: a MANDATORY record/ledger coherence check whose detection bound must be stated.** Exhibited by a **shipped test**, not hypothesized: `test_a_divergent_second_run_start_capture_is_not_read_as_completion` (`harness-runtime/tests/test_u_mem_26_write_boundary.py:1602-1662`) — a divergent second `capture_run_start` for one `run_id`, reachable because `EpisodicMemoryCapture` is a **public API** and `LocalAutomaticMemoryRuntime`'s presence guard is not in it, **overwrites `run.json`** (`memory_capture.py:730`) and only *then* meets the first call's row (`:732`). The capture correctly returns `FAILED`, but the durable residue is a record holding **call two's** content beside a single ledger row holding **call one's** `provider`; reconstructing the (ii-a) key against that record still resolves to exactly **one** `CAPTURE` row with a non-`None` provider, so every qualification above passes and the join returns **stale provenance as if determined**. None of cases (1)-(5) catches it. **What is checkable, exactly:** no field on the capture row binds it to a stored-content *version* — `MemoryOperationEntry` (`harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_operation_ledger.py:136-154`) carries no content hash; the `response_hash` inherited from `StateLedgerEntry` (`state_ledger_entry_schema.py:164-169`) is computed over **the ledger entry's own** canonical payload (`compute_memory_operation_response_hash`, `:335-338`, over `canonicalize_memory_operation`, `:324-332`); and `memory_refs` carries a `run_id`-derived, content-independent `memory_id`. So *"bind the row to the stored content hash"* **is not available at HEAD** — the field does not exist. What *is* available is the set of values `_capture` threads from **one call** into **both** surfaces: `entry.timestamp` ↔ `record.envelope.created_at` (to `_record` at `:649-658` and `_operation_payload` at `:694-707`), `entry.cli_profile` ↔ `content["cli_profile"]`, `entry.engine_class` ↔ `content["engine_class"]` (`_engine_class_value`, `:1122-1125`), `entry.step_id` ↔ `content["step_id"]` on the kinds that carry one, and `entry.run_id` ↔ `content["run_id"]` (vacuous — the key already derives from it). The `created_at` comparison must be scoped to **dispatch** rows (`repair_capture_operation` takes the repairer's own `datetime.now(UTC)`, `automatic_memory.py:442`), which costs nothing since a repair row is case (5) already. **Codex R8 [P2-1] correction, upheld by direct read at `d9907c24` and stated rather than quietly repaired — that comparable-field set is PER-KIND and the check is owed on `EPISODIC_RUN` ALONE.** Only `capture_run_start` (`memory_capture.py:309-320`) and `capture_run_close` (`:354-365`) write `cli_profile` and `engine_class` into **content**; `capture_turn_completion` (`:407-422`), `capture_tool_event` (`:459-470`), `capture_provider_route` (`:504-517`), `capture_failure_observation` (`:553-568`) and `capture_compaction_event` (`:604-614`) write neither — for those kinds both values reach the **ledger row only** (`_operation_payload`, `:1008-1009`) — while `step_id` is content-side on the turn / tool kinds and `None` on **both** surfaces for the run kinds (`:328` / `:377`) and compaction (`:622`). Stated kind-blind, the check false-`UNVERIFIABLE`s the entire non-run corpus against content fields those writers never store. **And the window it exists to close is `EPISODIC_RUN`-only anyway:** `_memory_id_for` (`:1100-1109`) derives every non-run kind's id from `content_hash` and `capture_operation_action_id(event_kind, memory_id)` (`:68-77`) composes the join key from that id — so there **the key IS the content binding**, precisely what R7 found missing for `EPISODIC_RUN`; a divergent re-capture is a *different* `memory_id` with its own row, and row and stored version cannot come apart. Every content-side comparator is therefore vacuous by construction there, leaving `created_at` — which **false-positives**: `timestamp` is not in the ledger's equivalence payload (`_equivalence_payload_from_payload`, `harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_operation_ledger.py:499-519`; eighteen keys, `provider` at `:511`, no `timestamp`), so a benign identical-content re-capture at a later instant returns `IDEMPOTENT_NOOP` while re-writing the record with a new `created_at`, and the three JSONL-backed episodic kinds read **last-match-wins** (`_read_jsonl_record`, `harness-is/src/harness_is/memory_store.py:445-463`; `_JSONL_BY_KIND` at `:143-147`). The genuinely divergent case never gets there — the equivalence payload *does* carry `provider`, so a same-content re-capture on a different provider raises `MemoryOperationIdempotencyConflictError` and, not being `RUN_START_EVENT_KIND`, re-raises into `FAILED` (`memory_capture.py:796-802`). **So the contract text is:** mandatory on `EPISODIC_RUN`, comprising `envelope.created_at` ↔ `entry.timestamp` (dispatch rows only), `content["cli_profile"]` ↔ `entry.cli_profile` and `content["engine_class"]` ↔ `entry.engine_class` (on the enum's `.value`, `_engine_class_value`, `:1122-1125`); **not owed on any other kind**. The residual disposition (α/β/γ) is likewise `EPISODIC_RUN`-scoped, which *sharpens* β — it is visibly the whole affected corpus, not a subset. **And the honest bound: this check does not close the case that produced it.** The witness's two calls differ in `workflow_id` / `provider` / `model` / `policy_ref` and agree on `cli_profile`, `engine_class` and `timestamp`; `provider` / `model` / `policy_ref` / `procedural_snapshot_ref` are **ledger-only** and `workflow_id` / `thread_id` / `provider_route` / `started_at` / `close_status` are **content-only**, so a divergence confined to either set is invisible to any comparison the two surfaces support. **The spec leg therefore owes an explicit disposition of the residual**, in one of three forms, none free: **(α)** add the record's `content_hash` (or an envelope-version stamp) to the capture row — a **C-MEM-08 closed-schema field addition**, i.e. exactly the schema cost (ii) was preferred for avoiding, and it repairs no already-written row; **(β)** classify every `EPISODIC_RUN` record `UNVERIFIABLE` by rule, which is (ii-b) applied to that kind and carries its stated foreclosure; or **(γ)** state the residual as **accepted**. **(i-envelope) has no counterpart residual** — its field ships *inside* the version it describes. **(ii-b) fail closed on ambiguity** — define no event-qualified key and map any multi-row resolution to `UNVERIFIABLE`. It invents no join semantics, but it is **not** the conservative default it looks like: it makes **every** `EPISODIC_RUN` record permanently unverifiable the moment `capture_run_close` acquires a production caller, i.e. it silently becomes reading **C** for run records while presenting itself as reading B, and must be taken as a stated foreclosure. The two are **not exclusive**: (ii-a) is the join, (ii-b) is its residue. Its real costs are therefore a **new cross-contract dependency** (C-MEM-10 on C-MEM-08 availability, plus an unindexed scan of `durable/memory_ops.jsonl` — no index over `memory_refs` *or* `action_id`) and an **unresolvable set each round makes larger — R3 took it from one case to three, R4 to five, R7 to six**: (1) no reachable capture row (promotion-, compaction-, native-adapter-authored, or unreadable ledger); (2) `stored_capture_event_type` returning `None` — deliberately strict, *"an unrecognizable record, never a guess at one"* — so the record cannot name its own writer; (3) the composed **key** matching no row (a torn capture whose repair never ran, `memory_capture.py:721-732`); **(4) [R4]** the matched row is **not** a `CAPTURE` row (`operation_kind != MemoryOperationKind.CAPTURE`) — the cross-producer `action_id` namespacing convention did not hold, and the ledger never guaranteed it would; **(5) [R4]** the matched row **is** a capture row but carries `provider = None`, which is the torn-capture **repair** row's shape *by construction* (`repair_capture_operation` passes `provider=None` / `model=None`, `memory_capture.py:938-939`, deliberately — *"a repair row attests that this capture is durable, not which dispatch performed it"*, `:916-921`), so a **repaired** capture yields *unknown* provenance and must not read as *same-family* merely because a row was found; **(6) [R7, scoped at R8]** the matched row **fails the record/ledger coherence check** (above) — row and stored record disagree on a value only one call could have written. **`EPISODIC_RUN` only** (`envelope.created_at` ↔ `entry.timestamp` on dispatch rows, `content["cli_profile"]`, `content["engine_class"]`; **not** `step_id`, `None` on both surfaces there — and the other kinds' join key is itself the content binding, so the failure mode cannot arise) — so the row is about a *different version* of this record and its `provider` is not this content's provenance; **detection-bounded, not exhaustive**, per the residual disposition above. Plus the ambiguity arm: **any** resolution returning more than one row is `UNVERIFIABLE` — **mandatory in the contract regardless**, vacuous only for the exact key-shaped (ii-a) join, since any join keyed on `action_id` or on `memory_refs` carries no single-row guarantee at all. All of them must be specified rather than defaulted — reuse the B-91 tri-state idiom verbatim (`MATCH` / `CONFIRMED_MISMATCH` / `UNVERIFIABLE`; `UNVERIFIABLE` → review-required, never auto-promote).
- **Recommendation: reading B [MODERATE] on Q1. On Q2 the recommendation is WITHDRAWN at R7 — the comparison has LEVELED and is presented as a two-option decision, not a pick, with a payload sub-choice under (i) added at R8.** *(R8 tested the leveling and left it standing: its two findings refine one column each — (ii)'s coherence check narrowed to `EPISODIC_RUN`, (i)'s boolean payload de-priced — and cancel on the balance. **R9 tested it again and it held again:** [P2-1] struck (i)'s write-side reach, but on the one axis (ii) had already won at zero, leaving the three axes that carry (ii)'s side untouched.)* Through R6 the pick was **(ii)** with the **(ii-a)** event-qualified join, held in place by **one** asymmetry: that (i-envelope) resolved the multi-writer kind by silent last-writer-wins accident where (ii-a) resolved it deliberately. **R7 [P2] found that asymmetry false** (envelope and content are co-written from one call, so the field describes the version it ships inside — (ii-a)'s semantics reached *directly*), and **R7 [P1] found the reverse asymmetry real** (nothing on a capture row binds it to a stored-content version, so a divergent second writer leaves (ii-a) reading stale provenance from a correctly-qualified single row — a case (i-envelope) cannot have). Having said at R6 that a struck-out cost *would* move the balance and that this row would say so, it says so. **The leveled ledger.** **(i-envelope)**: same-call provenance semantics that cannot go stale relative to the version they describe; no identity movement; no cross-contract dependency; a one-field read side — *costs* a closed-schema amendment at C-MEM-03 reversing v1.1's own zero-new-field posture one delta later, a second family-valued field beside `MemoryScope.provider_family`, and it is **forward-only** (no help for already-written records absent a back-fill, which is option (ii) offline) — the *"every capture writer touched"* cost previously listed here is **struck at R9 [P2-1]** as false. **(ii)/(ii-a)**: read-side only *as specified*, works on already-written records, and is the **only** option that can answer **aggregate-run** provenance (every capture row for the run is retained with its own `provider`; an envelope carries one writer's value only) — *costs* a new cross-contract read dependency plus an unindexed scan, **five** owed contract sentences (up from four), a mandatory coherence check whose detection bound must be stated, and an explicit disposition of the residual it cannot reach, of which **(α)** is itself a C-MEM-08 closed-schema addition — so "read-side only" is true of (ii-a) *as specified*, not of every honest closure of it. **What would decide it, and what this filing declines to invent:** the two columns answer *different questions* and the requirement has never been stated. **Stored-version provenance** ("which dispatch produced the content I am promoting") leaves them semantically equivalent on the coherent path with (i-envelope) strictly more reliable on the incoherent one; **aggregate-run provenance** ("did any leg of this run run cross-family") *requires* (ii), and adopting that reading is itself a decision this filing has not argued for. Q2 confidence stays **[MODERATE]** and is now confidence in the *framing* rather than in a pick: this comparison has been corrected at R3 (wrong field family), R4 (wrong uniqueness guarantee), R6 (variant-blind cost) and twice at R7, once on each side. What the filing carries forward is the mechanics **both** options must respect — state the key exactly and what enforces its uniqueness; state the coherence check and its detection bound; state the residual's disposition; and under (i) state hash-inertness and the forward-only consequence. The B-86 council record decides it in two directions at once. It **forecloses C**: C3's continuity argument (candidate-side partitioning *"makes the moment accumulated context matters most (the fallback leg) the moment it disappears — silent, permanent, inverted-in-timing data loss"*) and C6's *"a fallback chain is a **continuity** abstraction, not a substitution abstraction"* refinement apply *a fortiori* to a promotion refusal. It does **not license A's silence**: C10 classified `memory.write_note` `write-bounded-irreversible` precisely because it is *"durable, primary-attributed, promotion-eligible"* — naming promotion-eligibility itself as part of the danger — and declined to re-key the scope only because the *mechanism* was wrong (*"the defect is in the absent gate at the dispatch boundary, not in the scope's key"*). Reading B is that same resolution shape one contract downstream: keep the partition (C3/C6), put the gate where it belongs (C10). **The R4 correction strengthens exactly this half:** capture is durable but **not injectable**, and promotion to an `ACTIVE` semantic/procedural record is exactly and only the step that makes content injectable into a future run — so the threat model's *"Cross-run prompt-injection persistence through promoted semantic/procedural memory."* bullet is not *adjacent* to this question: **promotion is the whole of that channel**, and C-MEM-10 is the contract that governs it. The decisive asymmetry against A is that the pipeline today is permissive **by blindness**, not by policy, on the production composed-scope path (the residual path, which can see the producing family, does not pose the question), so "eligible, unchanged" would convert a structural gap into a contract commitment the policy layer never evaluated — the exact defect the v1.1 change-note named as B-86's own trigger (**"Why this is conformance repair, not design extension (X-AL-3)"**). **Confidence on Q1 stays MODERATE, not HIGH — the R1 exposure corrections *lowered* it, and R4 is the first round that pushed *upward* without moving it.** B costs a discriminator A does not, and the exposure that discriminator buys down is **nil at HEAD**, not merely bounded; B therefore rests entirely on the *contract* argument, which R1 leaves unweakened and R4 *strengthens* (it was never that content is leaking today). The tag was never set by doubt about that argument but by the cost/exposure asymmetry, which R4 leaves intact — so what moves is **A-as-permanent** (now weaker, having lost its "no new reach" rationale), while **A-as-interim is untouched**, resting as it does on the exposure fact rather than on the corrected rationale. **Consequence, stated rather than steered:** **A-as-interim is now materially more defensible** — with zero live exposure it costs nothing observable, while B's discriminator is real work against a path nothing exercises — *provided* two conditions are recorded with it: (a) an interim with **B named as the target**, not a discharge; and (b) the interim-ending triggers named **as the combinations they actually are** (the R6 [P2-2] sharpening — they remain **review-triggers**, any one of which re-opens the question, but each is now recorded with the transition it enables so no later reader infers that one alone turns on live auto-promotion): **T1** a policy admitting a capture-written kind into `eligible_record_kinds` → **candidate-reachability**, a durable retrieval-excluded `PROPOSED` corpus that a later family-term-free operator flip activates; **T2** the decision moving to `PROMOTE_SEMANTIC`/`PROMOTE_PROCEDURAL` → **active auto-promotion of already-eligible kinds**, live *immediately* since `ReviewMode.AUTOMATIC` is already the production setting (`automatic_memory.py:555`), i.e. the only trigger one change away from real promotions today; **T1 ∧ T2** → the full **first-order** exposure, at which point the interim's premise is false; **T3** the first caller to populate `promotion_candidates` → **hint-path candidate-reachability only** (it promotes nothing by itself: under `PROPOSE_SEMANTIC` `_auto_promote_allowed` is False, and no actuator reads it), but it forces the undefined candidate-family convention and would naturally arrive with the actuator that does not exist today. Absent (b) the interim silently becomes permanent exactly when it stops being safe — and the single-change framing would have let T1 or T2 pass later as "not the live one yet". B remains the recommendation on the merits; A-as-recorded-interim is now a close second rather than a concession. What must not happen is still a second round of silence.
- **Ratification ask.** **Q1** — A, B, or C (or "A as recorded interim, B as target", recorded with the **T1 / T2 / T1 ∧ T2 / T3** triggers above rather than with a two-item list, so each transition is named). **Q2, only if B or C — a LEVELED two-option decision, presented without a recommendation** *(changed at R7; the A/B/C reading recommendation is untouched — it never rested on Q2)*. **(i-envelope)** — a new hash-inert provenance field on `MemoryRecordEnvelope`: same-call semantics that cannot go stale relative to the stored version, no identity movement, no cross-contract dependency; costs a closed-schema C-MEM-03 amendment and is forward-only (the write-side reach previously listed here is struck at R9 [P2-1]: one central derivation in `_record`, no public signature change) — **with a payload sub-choice added at R8**: **(i-envelope-bool)** (a tri-state `captured_cross_family`) adds no value domain and no second family-valued field and is the cheapest shape, but answers present/absent only; **(i-envelope-family)** adds the family value, supports a future family-discriminating policy, and pays the value domain plus a second family-valued field beside `MemoryScope.provider_family`. *(Variant (i-content) is strictly dominated by it — same benefits plus identity movement.)* **(ii)/(ii-a)** — the event-qualified C-MEM-08 ledger join: the row is selected by the capture **`idempotency_key`** (`idempotent:capture:{event_kind}:{memory_id}`, composed from the record's own declared `event_type`), **not** by `memory_refs`, which cannot discriminate the run-start / run-close rows sharing one `EPISODIC_RUN` `memory_id`, and **not** by the bare `action_id`, which the ledger does not dedup on; the matched row is qualified on `operation_kind == CAPTURE`, and the **six**-case `UNVERIFIABLE` residue plus the multi-match arm fail closed — review-required, never auto-promote; works on already-written records and is the only option that answers aggregate-run provenance; costs a cross-contract read dependency plus an unindexed scan, five owed sentences, the mandatory coherence check with its detection bound, and the residual disposition (α/β/γ) of which α is itself a C-MEM-08 schema addition. Sub-choice **(ii-b)** — fail closed on *any* multi-row ambiguity — is available and needs no new join semantics, but is a de-facto reading **C** for `EPISODIC_RUN` records and must be taken as a stated foreclosure. **What would decide it:** state the requirement — **stored-version** provenance favours (i-envelope); **aggregate-run** provenance *requires* (ii). **A durable contract commitment** — once C-MEM-10 states a position, records accumulate against it, and promotion supersession does not delete (C-MEM-03 §Invariants, *"Supersession does not delete the prior record."*). **Codex R9 [P2-2] correction, upheld by direct read at `26720e8e`:** the pre-R9 text said *"irreversible"*, with reversal leaving the corpus *"with no way to re-adjudicate the already-promoted records"* — **too strong.** `_semantic_record_content` persists lineage (`"source_memory_refs"`, `memory_promotion.py:919-943`, field at `:932`) and capture rows retain their `provider` — exactly what option (ii) reads, and option (ii) works on already-written records — so a later migration **can** traverse lineage → row → provider and supersede or deny promotions made under a reversed position. **Reversal is costly and INCOMPLETE, not impossible**, bounded by this filing's own unresolvable set: **missing** rows (cases 1/3 — no reachable row, or a torn capture whose repair never ran), **repaired** rows (case 5 — `provider = None` by construction, `memory_capture.py:938-939`), **stale** rows (case 6, `EPISODIC_RUN`-scoped per R8 — a matched row describing a superseded version, detected only within the coherence check's own bound); **plus a fourth bound on the traversal**: `_procedural_record_content` (`:959-989`) writes `evidence` but **no** `source_memory_refs`, so promoted **procedural** records carry no lineage at all. **The gate stands on a narrower ground:** the cheapest moment to decide is *before* durable records accumulate — every promotion under a later-reversed position becomes a row in an unindexed, partly-blind, non-deleting migration that cannot recall content already injected into a completed run. **RATIFIED 2026-07-29 (operator `AskUserQuestion`, autonomous-loop session; root `CLAUDE.md` §14.2 — the interactive menu is this workspace's ratification primitive; §12.4.1 — the loop drives an item to its **genuine** gate and surfaces exactly that). Q1 = READING B, "flag + gate":** cross-family-produced candidates carry a risk flag **and** a review gate (`review_required`, never `auto_promote_allowed`) — **eligibility PRESERVED** (reading C's outright refusal foreclosed, and with it the harm the B-86 council named — C3's *"silent, permanent, inverted-in-timing data loss"*, C6's continuity abstraction) and **the silence REMOVED** (reading A not adopted as the permanent posture). Per fork §4's own cost line the ratified position **states the gate, not only the flag**. The **A-as-recorded-interim** variant was **not** selected, so its T1/T2/T3 interim-ending triggers are not owed. **Q2 (the discriminator mechanism) was deliberately NOT decided** — the selected option's own text routes it: *"spec leg picks the mechanism from the filing's leveled Q2 analysis"*, the same disposition fork §10's cap already made binding and which this ratification leaves unchanged; the option + payload sub-choice, the join key and its `operation_kind == CAPTURE` qualification, the six-case `UNVERIFIABLE` set + multi-match arm, the coherence check with its `EPISODIC_RUN` scope and detection bound, the α/β/γ residual, (i)'s placement / payload / derivation rule / tri-state / hash-inertness, and the missing **requirement** statement are **all the spec leg's**. §8's discriminator guidance stands as *input, not decision*: reading B's requirement as this filing argues it is stored-version provenance **present/absent**, answered most cheaply by **(i-envelope-bool)** — a recommendation the spec leg **re-derives** against its own HEAD, not a ratified choice. **Status STAYS `design_substrate_gated`** — the ratification answers §8, it does not run the spec leg (this register's own status enum: *"a filed doc does NOT flip the status — only ratified + applied spec deltas"*); the row flips to `open` when the C-MEM + plan deltas are APPLIED. **Next-step head: open the SPEC-WRITER APPLY LEG as its own arc/PR** — the C-MEM-10 amendment (risk-flag vocabulary sentence — `risk_flags` is an **open** `list<string>`, so a vocabulary addition and **not** a schema widening — plus the gate-bearing obligation that makes the flag non-inert) + the discriminator's owed contract text at C-MEM-03 (option (i)) or C-MEM-08 (option (ii)) + a **new U-MEM-27** memory-plan delta + clearance markers in the same PR + an out-of-family `just codex-review` decorator on the C-MEM diff; the **impl leg** follows as its own arc, per the **B-86 → PR #1147 (spec) → PR #1148 (impl)** precedent chain — spec first, impl second, never bundled. **Run the spec leg as a FRESH, FOCUSED session:** fork §10's cap *requires* it to re-ground every mechanics claim (all pinned to code at `26720e8e`) against its **own** HEAD before authoring contract text (the B-86 → U-MEM-26 precedent), and a session carrying this filing's nine review rounds forward inherits precisely the claims the cap requires be re-derived. Recorded in full at the fork doc's **new §11**.

- **Review status — SOUNDNESS EXIT declared at R9; the filing is CLOSED to further review rounds (fork §10).** Nine out-of-family rounds. **Stable across all nine:** the three A/B/C readings and their owed spec text, the **Q1 recommendation of B** at [MODERATE], the A-as-recorded-interim option with its T1/T2/T3 triggers, the §1 witness, the routing, and the **ratification ask as an ask**. **What moved:** only the Q2 mechanics comparison — **eight** corrections, **one** verdict change (R7's leveling) — which the filing now explicitly declines to decide, and at R9 the *precision* of the ask's justification. **Why R9 was admitted after R8 declared itself the last round the rule admits:** [P2-2] landed on the **ratification ask**, one of the three surfaces the exit condition names as live and the first finding since R4 to reach one; [P2-1] is a Q2-mechanics finding that would otherwise be **registered** against the spec leg, applied alongside it only under R8's own false-statement standard. **The exit is on soundness, not review-quiet.** All remaining sub-altitude mechanics are the **spec leg's by rule** — the Q2 option + payload choice, the join key and its qualification, the six-case `UNVERIFIABLE` set + multi-match arm, the coherence check with its `EPISODIC_RUN` scope and detection bound, the α/β/γ residual disposition, (i)'s placement/payload/derivation-rule/tri-state/hash-inertness text, and the missing **requirement** statement that would decide between the options; the spec leg re-grounds every one against its own HEAD (the B-86 → U-MEM-26 precedent). **Any further finding — including a factually correct one — is to be registered against `B-92` for the spec leg, not applied to the filing**; the sole exception is a finding against an A/B/C **reading**, the **Q1 recommendation**, or the **ask itself**, none of which nine rounds has touched.
